B2: We've only opened today.

A3: Yes, much fresher since my last visit.

B4: What would you like?

A5: My usual. Let's see whether you've changed that too.

B4: I'll hold the tickets while you drink.

A5: Put them down. Tea first. If we start doing two steps together, we'll spend the whole journey wondering what we missed.

A1: This marriage certificate has the dot above the wrong letter.

B2: We've been married twenty years.

A3: Twenty years together. But until that dot moves, this doesn't certify a marriage.

B4: Then move it. Our anniversary party starts at seven.

A5: Certainly. But we'll need a wedding cake. This dot makes tonight our first day.

Readiness: evidence-complete-awaiting-fresh-review. Origin: original. Local candidate score: 90.
